12:T626e,import { Callout, Tabs } from 'nextra/components'

# State Management Guide

## When to Use State Management

State management is essential for:
- Tracking plugin progress and counters
- Caching expensive computations or API responses
- Sharing data between plugin executions
- Coordinating between multiple plugins (global_state)
- Implementing rate limiting and throttling
- Managing distributed locks and flags

<Callout type="info">
**Key Difference**: `state` is private to each plugin, while `global_state` is shared across all plugins.
</Callout>

## Quick Start

### Basic Operations
```javascript
// MVEL - Store and retrieve plugin state
state.put("last_processed_item", itemId);
state.put("request_count", 1000);

lastItem = state.get("last_processed_item");
if (state.containsKey("request_count")) {
    count = state.get("request_count");
}
```

```java
// Java - Store and retrieve plugin state (state() from the PluginContext)
State<String, Object> state = state();
state.put("last_processed_item", itemId);
state.put("request_count", 1000);

Object lastItem = state.get("last_processed_item");
if (state.containsKey("request_count")) {
    Object count = state.get("request_count");
}
```

### Atomic Counter
```javascript
// JavaScript - Thread-safe counter
const requestCount = state.increment("api_requests");
if (requestCount > 1000) {
    console.log("Milestone: 1000 API requests processed!");
}
```

```java
// Java - Thread-safe counter (increment is atomic)
long requestCount = state().increment("api_requests");
if (requestCount > 1000) {
    System.out.println("Milestone: 1000 API requests processed!");
}
```

## Core Concepts

### state vs global_state

| Feature | `state` | `global_state` |
|---------|---------|----------------|
| **Scope** | Plugin-specific | Shared across all plugins |
| **Isolation** | Complete - other plugins can't access | None - all plugins can read/write |
| **Use Cases** | Plugin config, progress tracking | Cross-plugin coordination, shared cache |
| **Namespace** | Automatic (plugin name) | Global namespace |
| **Java access** | `state()` | `globalState()` |

### Operation Categories

1. **Basic Operations** - Simple key-value storage
2. **Atomic Counters** - Thread-safe numeric operations
3. **Set Operations** - Manage collections atomically
4. **Compute Operations** - Update values based on current state
5. **Compare-and-Set** - Conditional updates for coordination

## API Reference

### Basic Operations

<Tabs items={['MVEL', 'JavaScript', 'Python', 'Java']}>
<Tabs.Tab>
```javascript
// Store values
state.put("key", "value");
state.put("counter", 42);
state.put("data", complexObject);

// Retrieve values
value = state.get("key");
counter = state.get("counter", 0); // With default

// Check existence
if (state.containsKey("key")) {
    // Key exists
}

// Remove values
state.remove("key");

// Clear all state (use with caution!)
state.clear();
```
</Tabs.Tab>
<Tabs.Tab>
```javascript
// Store values
state.put("key", "value");
state.put("counter", 42);
state.put("data", complexObject);

// Retrieve values
const value = state.get("key");
const counter = state.get("counter") || 0; // With default

// Check existence
if (state.containsKey("key")) {
    // Key exists
}

// Remove values
state.remove("key");

// Clear all state (use with caution!)
state.clear();
```
</Tabs.Tab>
<Tabs.Tab>
```python
# Store values
state.put("key", "value")
state.put("counter", 42)
state.put("data", complex_object)

# Retrieve values
value = state.get("key")
counter = state.get("counter", 0)  # With default

# Check existence
if state.containsKey("key"):
    # Key exists
    pass

# Remove values
state.remove("key")

# Clear all state (use with caution!)
state.clear()
```
</Tabs.Tab>
<Tabs.Tab>
```java
// Store values (state() returns a State<String, Object>)
State<String, Object> state = state();
state.put("key", "value");
state.put("counter", 42);
state.put("data", complexObject);

// Retrieve values (State#get has no default-value overload — compute one inline)
String value = (String) state.get("key");
long counter = state.containsKey("counter") ? ((Number) state.get("counter")).longValue() : 0L;

// Check existence
if (state.containsKey("key")) {
    // Key exists
}

// Remove values
state.remove("key");

// Clear all state (use with caution!)
state.clear();
```
</Tabs.Tab>
</Tabs>

### Atomic Counter Operations

These operations are thread-safe and perfect for concurrent environments:

<Tabs items={['MVEL', 'JavaScript', 'Python', 'Java']}>
<Tabs.Tab>
```javascript
// Increment operations
count = state.increment("counter");        // +1, returns new value
count = state.increment("counter", 5);     // +5, returns new value

// Decrement operations
count = state.decrement("counter");        // -1, returns new value
count = state.decrement("counter", 3);     // -3, returns new value

// Add and get variations
newVal = state.addAndGet("counter", 10);   // Add 10, return new value
oldVal = state.getAndAdd("counter", 10);   // Return old value, then add 10
```
</Tabs.Tab>
<Tabs.Tab>
```javascript
// Increment operations
let count = state.increment("counter");        // +1, returns new value
count = state.increment("counter", 5);         // +5, returns new value

// Decrement operations
count = state.decrement("counter");            // -1, returns new value
count = state.decrement("counter", 3);         // -3, returns new value

// Add and get variations
const newVal = state.addAndGet("counter", 10); // Add 10, return new value
const oldVal = state.getAndAdd("counter", 10); // Return old value, then add 10
```
</Tabs.Tab>
<Tabs.Tab>
```python
# Increment operations
count = state.increment("counter")        # +1, returns new value
count = state.increment("counter", 5)     # +5, returns new value

# Decrement operations
count = state.decrement("counter")        # -1, returns new value
count = state.decrement("counter", 3)     # -3, returns new value

# Add and get variations
new_val = state.addAndGet("counter", 10)  # Add 10, return new value
old_val = state.getAndAdd("counter", 10)  # Return old value, then add 10
```
</Tabs.Tab>
<Tabs.Tab>
```java
State<String, Object> state = state();

// Increment operations (return long)
long count = state.increment("counter");        // +1, returns new value
count = state.increment("counter", 5);          // +5, returns new value

// Decrement operations
count = state.decrement("counter");            // -1, returns new value
count = state.decrement("counter", 3);          // -3, returns new value

// Add and get variations
long newVal = state.addAndGet("counter", 10);   // Add 10, return new value
long oldVal = state.getAndAdd("counter", 10);    // Return old value, then add 10
```
</Tabs.Tab>
</Tabs>

### Set Operations

Manage collections of unique elements atomically:

<Tabs items={['MVEL', 'JavaScript', 'Python', 'Java']}>
<Tabs.Tab>
```javascript
// Add elements to set
added = state.addToSet("processed_items", itemId);  // Returns true if new

// Remove elements
removed = state.removeFromSet("processed_items", itemId);

// Check membership
exists = state.containsInSet("processed_items", itemId);

// Get entire set (immutable view)
allItems = state.getSet("processed_items");

// Get set size
size = state.setSize("processed_items");
```
</Tabs.Tab>
<Tabs.Tab>
```javascript
// Add elements to set
const added = state.addToSet("processed_items", itemId);  // Returns true if new

// Remove elements
const removed = state.removeFromSet("processed_items", itemId);

// Check membership
const exists = state.containsInSet("processed_items", itemId);

// Get entire set (immutable view)
const allItems = state.getSet("processed_items");

// Get set size
const size = state.setSize("processed_items");
```
</Tabs.Tab>
<Tabs.Tab>
```python
# Add elements to set
added = state.addToSet("processed_items", item_id)  # Returns true if new

# Remove elements
removed = state.removeFromSet("processed_items", item_id)

# Check membership
exists = state.containsInSet("processed_items", item_id)

# Get entire set (immutable view)
all_items = state.getSet("processed_items")

# Get set size
size = state.setSize("processed_items")
```
</Tabs.Tab>
<Tabs.Tab>
```java
State<String, Object> state = state();

// Add elements to set (returns true if the element was newly added)
boolean added = state.addToSet("processed_items", itemId);

// Remove elements
boolean removed = state.removeFromSet("processed_items", itemId);

// Check membership
boolean exists = state.containsInSet("processed_items", itemId);

// Get entire set (immutable view of Set<Object>)
java.util.Set<Object> allItems = state.getSet("processed_items");

// Get set size
long size = state.setSize("processed_items");
```
</Tabs.Tab>
</Tabs>

### Advanced Compute Operations

Update values based on their current state:

<Tabs items={['MVEL', 'JavaScript', 'Python', 'Java']}>
<Tabs.Tab>
```javascript
// Compute - update based on current value
state.compute("max_amount", (key, current) -> {
    return Math.max(current ?: 0, newAmount);
});

// Merge - combine new value with existing
state.merge("total_volume", amount, (existing, new) -> {
    return (existing ?: 0) + new;
});

// Compare and set - atomic conditional update
success = state.compareAndSet("status", "pending", "processing");

// Get and set - return old value, set new
oldValue = state.getAndSet("flag", true);
```
</Tabs.Tab>
<Tabs.Tab>
```javascript
// Compute - update based on current value
state.compute("max_amount", (key, current) => {
    return Math.max(current || 0, newAmount);
});

// Merge - combine new value with existing
state.merge("total_volume", amount, (existing, newVal) => {
    return (existing || 0) + newVal;
});

// Compare and set - atomic conditional update
const success = state.compareAndSet("status", "pending", "processing");

// Get and set - return old value, set new
const oldValue = state.getAndSet("flag", true);
```
</Tabs.Tab>
<Tabs.Tab>
```python
# Compute - update based on current value
def update_max(key, current):
    return max(current or 0, new_amount)
state.compute("max_amount", update_max)

# Merge - combine new value with existing
def sum_values(existing, new):
    return (existing or 0) + new
state.merge("total_volume", amount, sum_values)

# Compare and set - atomic conditional update
success = state.compareAndSet("status", "pending", "processing")

# Get and set - return old value, set new
old_value = state.getAndSet("flag", True)
```
</Tabs.Tab>
<Tabs.Tab>
```java
State<String, Object> state = state();

// Compute - update based on current value (BiFunction: key, current -> newValue)
state.compute("max_amount", (key, current) -> Math.max(
        current == null ? 0 : ((Number) current).longValue(), newAmount));

// Merge - combine new value with existing (existing, incoming -> newValue)
state.merge("total_volume", amount, (existing, incoming) ->
        (existing == null ? 0L : ((Number) existing).longValue()) + ((Number) incoming).longValue());

// Compare and set - atomic conditional update (returns true if it swapped)
boolean success = state.compareAndSet("status", "pending", "processing");

// Get and set - return old value, set new
Object oldValue = state.getAndSet("flag", true);
```
</Tabs.Tab>
</Tabs>

## Common Patterns

### 1. Progress Tracking

```javascript
// JavaScript - Track processing progress
function processBlocks(blocks) {
    // Get last processed block
    let lastProcessed = state.get("last_block") || 0;

    blocks.forEach(block => {
        if (block.number <= lastProcessed) {
            return; // Skip already processed
        }

        // Process block
        processBlock(block);

        // Update progress atomically
        state.put("last_block", block.number);
        state.increment("blocks_processed");
    });

    // Log statistics
    const total = state.get("blocks_processed");
    console.log(`Total blocks processed: ${total}`);
}
```

```java
// Java - Track processing progress (state() from the PluginContext)
void processBlocks(List<Block> blocks) {
    State<String, Object> state = state();
    long lastProcessed = state.containsKey("last_block")
            ? ((Number) state.get("last_block")).longValue() : 0L;

    for (Block block : blocks) {
        if (block.getNumber() <= lastProcessed) {
            continue; // Skip already processed
        }

        // Process block
        processBlock(block);

        // Update progress atomically
        state.put("last_block", block.getNumber());
        state.increment("blocks_processed");
    }

    // Log statistics
    long total = state.get("blocks_processed") == null
            ? 0L : (long) state.get("blocks_processed");
    System.out.println("Total blocks processed: " + total);
}
```

### 2. Rate Limiting

```javascript
// MVEL - Implement rate limiting
function checkRateLimit(clientId) {
    key = "rate_limit:" + clientId;
    windowKey = "rate_window:" + clientId;

    currentWindow = System.currentTimeMillis() / 60000; // 1-minute windows
    lastWindow = state.get(windowKey, 0);

    if (currentWindow != lastWindow) {
        // New time window, reset counter
        state.put(windowKey, currentWindow);
        state.put(key, 1);
        return true;
    }

    // Same window, check limit
    requests = state.increment(key);
    return requests <= 100; // 100 requests per minute
}
```

```java
// Java - Implement rate limiting (state() from the PluginContext)
boolean checkRateLimit(String clientId) {
    State<String, Object> state = state();
    String key = "rate_limit:" + clientId;
    String windowKey = "rate_window:" + clientId;

    long currentWindow = System.currentTimeMillis() / 60_000L; // 1-minute windows
    long lastWindow = state.containsKey(windowKey)
            ? ((Number) state.get(windowKey)).longValue() : 0L;

    if (currentWindow != lastWindow) {
        // New time window, reset counter
        state.put(windowKey, currentWindow);
        state.put(key, 1L);
        return true;
    }

    // Same window, check limit
    long requests = state.increment(key);
    return requests <= 100; // 100 requests per minute
}
```

### 3. Deduplication

```javascript
// JavaScript - Prevent duplicate processing
function processTransaction(tx) {
    const txHash = tx.getTxHash();

    // Atomic check-and-add
    if (!state.addToSet("processed_transactions", txHash)) {
        console.log(`Transaction ${txHash} already processed, skipping`);
        return false;
    }

    try {
        // Process the transaction
        const result = doProcessing(tx);

        // Track successful processing
        state.increment("successful_txs");
        return true;

    } catch (error) {
        // Remove from set on failure to allow retry
        state.removeFromSet("processed_transactions", txHash);
        state.increment("failed_txs");
        throw error;
    }
}
```

```java
// Java - Prevent duplicate processing (state() from the PluginContext)
boolean processTransaction(Transaction tx) {
    State<String, Object> state = state();
    String txHash = tx.getTxHash();

    // Atomic check-and-add
    if (!state.addToSet("processed_transactions", txHash)) {
        System.out.println("Transaction " + txHash + " already processed, skipping");
        return false;
    }

    try {
        // Process the transaction
        doProcessing(tx);

        // Track successful processing
        state.increment("successful_txs");
        return true;
    } catch (RuntimeException error) {
        // Remove from set on failure to allow retry
        state.removeFromSet("processed_transactions", txHash);
        state.increment("failed_txs");
        throw error;
    }
}
```

### 4. Distributed Locking

```javascript
// MVEL - Simple distributed lock using compare-and-set
function acquireLock(lockName, timeout) {
    lockKey = "lock:" + lockName;
    lockHolder = Thread.currentThread().getName() + ":" + System.currentTimeMillis();

    // Try to acquire lock
    if (global_state.compareAndSet(lockKey, null, lockHolder)) {
        return lockHolder; // Lock acquired
    }

    // Check if existing lock is expired
    existing = global_state.get(lockKey);
    if (existing != null) {
        parts = existing.split(":");
        lockTime = Long.parseLong(parts[1]);

        if (System.currentTimeMillis() - lockTime > timeout) {
            // Lock expired, try to take over
            if (global_state.compareAndSet(lockKey, existing, lockHolder)) {
                return lockHolder;
            }
        }
    }

    return null; // Failed to acquire
}

function releaseLock(lockName, lockHolder) {
    lockKey = "lock:" + lockName;
    return global_state.compareAndSet(lockKey, lockHolder, null);
}
```

```java
// Java - Simple distributed lock using compare-and-set (globalState() from the PluginContext)
// Tip: for production locking prefer context().locker() over a hand-rolled CAS lock.
String acquireLock(String lockName, long timeoutMillis) {
    State<String, Object> globalState = globalState();
    String lockKey = "lock:" + lockName;
    String lockHolder = Thread.currentThread().getName() + ":" + System.currentTimeMillis();

    // Try to acquire lock
    if (globalState.compareAndSet(lockKey, null, lockHolder)) {
        return lockHolder; // Lock acquired
    }

    // Check if existing lock is expired
    String existing = (String) globalState.get(lockKey);
    if (existing != null) {
        String[] parts = existing.split(":");
        long lockTime = Long.parseLong(parts[1]);

        if (System.currentTimeMillis() - lockTime > timeoutMillis) {
            // Lock expired, try to take over
            if (globalState.compareAndSet(lockKey, existing, lockHolder)) {
                return lockHolder;
            }
        }
    }

    return null; // Failed to acquire
}

boolean releaseLock(String lockName, String lockHolder) {
    return globalState().compareAndSet("lock:" + lockName, lockHolder, null);
}
```

### 5. Caching with Expiry

```python
# Python - Cache with TTL
def get_cached_data(key, fetch_function, ttl_seconds=300):
    cache_key = f"cache:{key}"
    timestamp_key = f"cache_time:{key}"

    # Check cache validity
    cached_time = state.get(timestamp_key, 0)
    current_time = System.currentTimeMillis() / 1000

    if current_time - cached_time < ttl_seconds:
        # Cache is valid
        cached_data = state.get(cache_key)
        if cached_data:
            return cached_data

    # Fetch fresh data
    fresh_data = fetch_function()

    # Update cache atomically
    state.put(cache_key, fresh_data)
    state.put(timestamp_key, current_time)

    return fresh_data
```

```java
// Java - Cache with TTL (state() from the PluginContext)
Object getCachedData(String key, java.util.function.Supplier<Object> fetchFunction, long ttlSeconds) {
    State<String, Object> state = state();
    String cacheKey = "cache:" + key;
    String timestampKey = "cache_time:" + key;

    // Check cache validity
    long cachedTime = state.containsKey(timestampKey)
            ? ((Number) state.get(timestampKey)).longValue() : 0L;
    long currentTime = System.currentTimeMillis() / 1000L;

    if (currentTime - cachedTime < ttlSeconds) {
        // Cache is valid
        Object cachedData = state.get(cacheKey);
        if (cachedData != null) {
            return cachedData;
        }
    }

    // Fetch fresh data
    Object freshData = fetchFunction.get();

    // Update cache atomically
    state.put(cacheKey, freshData);
    state.put(timestampKey, currentTime);

    return freshData;
}
```

### 6. Metrics Aggregation

```javascript
// JavaScript - Aggregate metrics across events
function updateMetrics(event) {
    const amount = event.getAmount();

    // Update various metrics atomically
    state.increment("event_count");
    state.merge("total_amount", amount, (old, val) => (old || 0) + val);

    // Track maximum
    state.compute("max_amount", (key, current) => {
        return Math.max(current || 0, amount);
    });

    // Track unique addresses
    state.addToSet("unique_addresses", event.getAddress());

    // Update hourly buckets
    const hour = Math.floor(Date.now() / 3600000);
    state.increment(`hourly_count:${hour}`);
}

function getMetricsSummary() {
    return {
        total_events: state.get("event_count", 0),
        total_amount: state.get("total_amount", 0),
        max_amount: state.get("max_amount", 0),
        unique_addresses: state.setSize("unique_addresses")
    };
}
```

```java
// Java - Aggregate metrics across events (state() from the PluginContext)
void updateMetrics(Event event) {
    State<String, Object> state = state();
    long amount = event.getAmount();

    // Update various metrics atomically
    state.increment("event_count");
    state.merge("total_amount", amount, (old, val) ->
            (old == null ? 0L : ((Number) old).longValue()) + ((Number) val).longValue());

    // Track maximum
    state.compute("max_amount", (key, current) ->
            Math.max(current == null ? 0L : ((Number) current).longValue(), amount));

    // Track unique addresses
    state.addToSet("unique_addresses", event.getAddress());

    // Update hourly buckets
    long hour = System.currentTimeMillis() / 3_600_000L;
    state.increment("hourly_count:" + hour);
}

Map<String, Object> getMetricsSummary() {
    State<String, Object> state = state();
    // Note: Map.of rejects null values, and state.get() returns null before the first event —
    // use a HashMap (or null defaults) instead.
    Map<String, Object> summary = new HashMap<>();
    summary.put("total_events", state.get("event_count"));
    summary.put("total_amount", state.get("total_amount"));
    summary.put("max_amount", state.get("max_amount"));
    summary.put("unique_addresses", state.setSize("unique_addresses"));
    return summary;
}
```

## Best Practices

### 1. Use Atomic Operations for Counters

<Callout type="warning">
Always use atomic operations (`increment`, `decrement`) for counters instead of get/put to avoid race conditions.
</Callout>

```javascript
// ❌ BAD - Race condition risk
let count = state.get("counter") || 0;
state.put("counter", count + 1);

// ✅ GOOD - Atomic operation
const count = state.increment("counter");
```

```java
// BAD - Race condition risk
State<String, Object> state = state();
long count = state.containsKey("counter") ? ((Number) state.get("counter")).longValue() : 0L;
state.put("counter", count + 1);

// GOOD - Atomic operation
long atomicCount = state.increment("counter");
```

### 2. Namespace Your Keys

```javascript
// Good key naming prevents collisions
const keys = {
    lastBlock: "processor:last_block",
    apiCache: "cache:api:response",
    rateLimit: `rate:${clientId}:${window}`
};
```

```java
// Java - Good key naming prevents collisions
String LAST_BLOCK = "processor:last_block";
String API_CACHE = "cache:api:response";
String rateLimit = "rate:" + clientId + ":" + window;
```

### 3. Handle Missing Keys Gracefully

```javascript
// Always provide defaults or check existence
const config = state.get("config") || getDefaultConfig();
const retries = state.get("retry_count", 0);

if (state.containsKey("important_flag")) {
    // Process only if flag exists
}
```

```java
// Java - Always provide defaults or check existence (State#get has no default overload)
State<String, Object> state = state();
Object config = state.get("config");
if (config == null) config = getDefaultConfig();

if (state.containsKey("retry_count")) {
    long retries = ((Number) state.get("retry_count")).longValue();
}

if (state.containsKey("important_flag")) {
    // Process only if flag exists
}
```

### 4. Clean Up Stale Data

```javascript
// Periodically clean up old data
function cleanupOldData() {
    const cutoff = Date.now() - (24 * 60 * 60 * 1000); // 24 hours ago

    // Remove old cache entries
    const cacheKeys = state.getKeys().filter(k => k.startsWith("cache:"));
    cacheKeys.forEach(key => {
        const timestamp = state.get(key + ":time");
        if (timestamp && timestamp < cutoff) {
            state.remove(key);
            state.remove(key + ":time");
        }
    });
}
```

```java
// Java - Periodically clean up old data
// Note: State has no getKeys() enumerator; track keys you create in a set instead.
void cleanupOldData() {
    State<String, Object> state = state();
    long cutoff = System.currentTimeMillis() - (24 * 60 * 60 * 1000L); // 24 hours ago

    // Remove old cache entries for the keys this plugin manages
    for (String key : trackedCacheKeys) {
        Object ts = state.get(key + ":time");
        if (ts instanceof Number n && n.longValue() < cutoff) {
            state.remove(key);
            state.remove(key + ":time");
        }
    }
}
```


## Performance Considerations

- **Atomic operations** are fast and lock-free
- **Set operations** maintain uniqueness automatically
- **Large objects** in state can impact memory - consider storing IDs and fetching from database
